from re_arc.dsl import *
def generate_06df4c85() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}")
    print(f"diff_ub: {diff_ub}")

    cols = interval(0, 10, 1)
    print(f"cols: {cols}")

    oh = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, 4))
    print(f"oh: {oh}")

    ow = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, 4))
    print(f"ow: {ow}")

    numh = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (3, 31 // (oh + 1)))
    print(f"numh: {numh}")

    numw = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (3, 31 // (ow + 1)))
    print(f"numw: {numw}")

    bgc, linc = sample(cols, 2)
    print(f"bgc: {bgc}, linc: {linc}")

    remcols = difference(cols, (bgc, linc))
    print(f"remcols: {remcols}")

    ncols = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, min(8, (numh * numh) // 3)))
    print(f"ncols: {ncols}")

    ccols = sample(remcols, ncols)
    print(f"ccols: {ccols}")

    fullh = numh * oh + numh - 1
    print(f"fullh: {fullh}")

    fullw = numw * ow + numw - 1
    print(f"fullw: {fullw}")

    gi = canvas(linc, (fullh, fullw))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")

    sgi = asindices(canvas(bgc, (oh, ow)))
    print(f"sgi: {sgi}")

    for a in range(numh):
        for b in range(numw):
            gi = fill(gi, bgc, shift(sgi, (a * (oh + 1), b * (ow + 1))))
            print(f"gi after filling: {gi}")

    sinds = asindices(canvas(-1, (oh, ow)))
    print(f"sinds: {sinds}")

    for col in ccols:
        inds = occurrences(gi, recolor(bgc, sinds))
        print(f"inds for col {col}: {inds}")
        if len(inds) == 0:
            break
        loc = choice(totuple(inds))
        print(f"loc: {loc}")

        narms = randint(1, 4)
        print(f"narms: {narms}")

        armdirs = sample(totuple(dneighbors((0, 0))), narms)
        print(f"armdirs: {armdirs}")

        succ = 0
        for armdir in armdirs:
            x, y = armdir
            arm = []
            for k in range(1, max(numh, numw)):
                nextloc = add(loc, (k * x * (oh + 1), k * y * (ow + 1)))
                if nextloc not in inds:
                    break
                arm.append(nextloc)
            if len(arm) < 2:
                continue
            aidx = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, len(arm) - 1))
            endp = arm[aidx]
            gi = fill(gi, col, shift(sinds, endp))
            print(f"gi after filling with col {col}: {gi}")
            succ += 1

        gi = fill(gi, col, shift(sinds, loc))
        print(f"gi after final fill with col {col}: {gi}")

    return {'input': gi}
